Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in combinatorics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 137 and the invariant I(A,B) = 651 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 137 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the combinatorics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 651. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
